wolfvill,53,234,4	script	Transport Cart#ep18_1	4_EP18_WAGON,{
	if (ep18_main < 36)
		end;
	if (isbegin_quest(12532) == 1) {
		mes "^0000ffAunque it looks fine, hay un worn-out carriage wheel..^000000";
		next;
		if (select( "I attempt maintenance work", "I parar el maintenance work" ) == 2) {
			mes "^0000ffVamos a revisit esto cuando it comes to mente despues.^000000";
			close;
		}
		progressbar_npc "FFFF00",2;
		if (countitem(7197) < 1) {
			mes "Materials suitable para reinforcing el carriage wheel <ITEM>[Tough Vines]<INFO>7197</INFO></ITEM> unavailable.";
			close;
		}
		// note: Tough_Vines not deleted on fail
		.@r = rand(1,5);
		if (.@r == 1) {
			specialeffect EF_REFINEOK;
			mes "^0000ffRepair successful!^000000";
			delitem 7197,1;	// Tough_Vines
			removespecialeffect 1090;
			completequest 12532;
			close;
		}
		specialeffect EF_REFINEFAIL;
		mes "^0000ffNo parece to tener sido properly reinforced aun. El material no ha disappeared, asi que vamos a mantener intentando^000000";
		close;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(12532) == 1" );
	end;
}

	npctalk "Bueno grief", "Camille#ep18_2", bc_self;
	next;
	mes "^0000ffSeems como Soy unbout to collapse. Uncapaz de focus on donde to attack, my ojos son rolling sin restraint. Probablemente no voy a ultimo incluso a second, becoming an easy meal para el wolves^000000";
	next;
	while(1) {
		next;
		switch( select( "Dar combat advice to Camille", "I handle el wolves personaally", "Pretend no to notice y pass by" ) ) {
		case 1:
			mes "^0000ffTell Camille donde to attack el wolves?^000000";
			PlayBGM "182";
			while(1) {
				mes "Wolf vitality -^ff0000" + .@wolf_hp + "^000000";
				mes "Camille's vitality -^0000ff" + .@camille_hp + "^000000";
				next;
				switch( select( "Timid attack(High probability)", "Moderate attack(Normal probability)", "Strong attack(Low probability)", "Counterattack(Thin Probability)", "Run away for now" ) ) {
				case 1:
					.@wolf_chance = 65;	// 65% chance the wolf is being hit / 35% Camille
					.@wolf_damage = rand(25,50);
					.@camille_damage = rand(45,93);
					break;
				case 2:
					.@wolf_chance = 42;
					.@wolf_damage = rand(32,58);
					.@camille_damage = rand(37,72);
					break;
				case 3:
					.@wolf_chance = 30;
					.@wolf_damage = rand(45,70);
					.@camille_damage = rand(34,53);
					break;
				case 4:
					.@wolf_chance = 25;
					.@wolf_damage = rand(66,80);
					.@camille_damage = rand(26,50);
					break;
				case 5:
					mes "Soy upset, pero hay no oportunidad of winning ahora. Vamos a fix it y intentar de nuevo";
					close;
				}
				if (.@wolf_chance > rand(100)) {
					.@wolf_hp = .@wolf_hp - .@wolf_damage;
					npctalk "-" + .@wolf_damage, "#ep18_2_mon", bc_self;
					npctalk " ", "Camille#ep18_2", bc_self;
					specialeffect EF_ACIDDEMON, SELF, "#ep18_2_mon";
					specialeffect EF_HFLIMOON3, SELF, "#ep18_2_mon";
				}
				else {
					.@camille_hp = .@camille_hp - .@camille_damage;
					npctalk " ", "#ep18_2_mon", bc_self;
					npctalk "-" + .@camille_damage, "Camille#ep18_2", bc_self;
					specialeffect EF_ACIDDEMON, SELF, "Camille#ep18_2";
					specialeffect EF_HFLIMOON3, SELF, "Camille#ep18_2";
				}
				if (.@wolf_hp < 1) {
					mes "^0000ffTienes defeated el gray wolf.^000000";
					completequest 12534;
					cloakonnpcself( "#ep18_2_mon" );
					playbgm "183";
					close;
				}
				if (.@camille_hp < 1) {
					next;
					mes "^ff0000Dangerous!! Necesito parar el batalla y restore Camille's health.^000000";
					playbgm "183";
					close;
				}
			}
			break;
		case 2:
			mes "^0000ffNo, it podria be bien to solo ayudar Camille grow up on ella propio....^000000";
			continue;
		case 3:
			mes "^0000ffCome de vuelta despues y build Camille's grave.^000000";
			close;
		}
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(12534) == 1" );
	end;
}

gw_fild01,24,122,0	script	Trap Set#01	CLEAR_NPC,{
	if (checkweight(1000405,20) == 0) {
		mes "- Espera un momento !! -";
		mes "- Actualmente estas cargando -";
		mes "- demasiados objetos contigo. -";
		mes "- Por favor intenta de nuevo -";
		mes "- despues de soltar algo de peso. -";
		close;
	}
	if (ep18_main < 36)
		end;
	if (isbegin_quest(8688) == 1 || isbegin_quest(8690) == 1) {
		.@amount = countitem(1000412) + countitem(1000413) + countitem(1000414);
		if (.@amount >= 5) {
			mes "He collected suficiente things caught in el traps hoy.";
			mes "Vamos a traer it to <NAVI>[Eruin]<INFO>wolfvill,202,166,0,101,0</INFO></NAVI>.";
			close;
		}
		specialeffect2 EF_STEAL;
		progressbar "FFFF00",10;
		.@r = rand(1,5);
		if (.@r == 1)
			getitem 1000412,1;	// Ep18_Trapped01
		else if (.@r == 2)
			getitem 1000413,1;	// Ep18_Trapped02
		else if (.@r == 3)
			getitem 1000414,1;	// Ep18_Trapped03
		else
			mes "I no obtener algo...";
		disablenpc strnpcinfo(0);
		initnpctimer;
		end;
	}
	end;
OnTimer60000:
	enablenpc strnpcinfo(0);
	stopnpctimer;
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_CLICKME, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(8688) == 1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_CLICKME, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(8690) == 1" );
	end;
}

			mes "[Ahir]";
			mes "Okay, it probablemente no voy a ultimo long.";
			mes "Va a increase again manana, but Espero que will come again after dawn and catch a " + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + ".";
			next;
			mes "[Ahir]";
			mes "Este es un gracias para letting me sleep bueno esta noche.";
			mes "Please aceptar incluso a small corazon, aventurero.";

			setquest 16550;

			getexp 7769124,3000000;
			mes " ";
			mes "^4d4dffA favorable rating para tu occurs.^000000";
			add_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18, 100);
			if (get_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18) >= 1000 && ep18_main == 36) {// Custom implementation: reputation 1000 on wolfvill => player can continue the main quest
				ep18_main = 37;
				setquest 18082;
				mes "^4d4dffThe aldears of el gray wolf tener accepted tu as part of el aldea.^000000";
				mes "Eres eligible to participate in el^4d4dffconference.^000000";
			}
			close;
		}
	}
	switch( checkquest(16550,PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "Parece que the " + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + " is todavia lurking.";
		mes "Si provoke me ahora, it podria be mas noisy debido a evil.";
		next;

		mes "I beg tu, Aventurero.";
		close;
	case 2:
		erasequest 16550;
		break;
	}
	switch( checkquest(16549,HUNTING) ) {
	case -1:
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "Sigh, Soy asi que sleepy de nuevo hoy.";
		mes "Adventurer, would you mind catching solo 30 " + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + " for me?";
		next;
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "Si hunt in a group, the " + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + " will probablemente be silent esta noche.";
		next;
		if (select( "Para seguro, Voy a catch ellos all.", "No estoy interesado to catch ellos." ) == 2) {
			mes "[Ahir]";
			mes "Bueno, I suppose eres busy tambien, Aventurero?";
			mes "Debes ser tired, asi que ir ahead y obtener algunos rest. Podria ser hard to sleep con all esto chipred, though.";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "Wow, gracias, Aventurero!";
		mes "Es 30 " + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + "s, I really appreciate it.";
		next;
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "Despues tiene hunted ellos all, por favor regresa y dejar me saber. Voy a preparar a small recompensa para tu!";
		setquest 16549;
		close;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "Gracias, Aventurero!";
		mes "Hay 30 " + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + "s. Please, take care of them.";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "Gracias! El chirping sound of el windstorm kite tiene definitivamente sido reduced!";
		mes "Puedo sleep comfortably esta noche.";
		next;
		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "Okay, it probablemente no voy a ultimo long.";
		mes "Va a increase again manana, but Espero que will come again after dawn and catch a " + getmonsterinfo("EP18_FIREWIND_KITE", MOB_NAME) + ".";

		mes "[Ahir]";
		mes "Este es un gracias para letting me sleep bueno esta noche.";
		mes "Please aceptar incluso a small corazon, aventurero.";

		setquest 16550;

			.@bonus = 1;

		}

		getexp 10318080,3000000;
		close;
	}
	end;

	npctalk "Ahir: Soy realmente tired... y necesitar mas sleep.", "Ahir#ep18", bc_self;
	end;

	// first completion
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16548,HUNTING) == -1 && isbegin_quest(11724) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16548,HUNTING) == 2" );

	// daily

	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16549,HUNTING) == 2" );
	end;

rachel,176,80,0	script	#Hoesopflowerstart	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,10,10,{
	end;
OnTouch:
	if (BaseLevel < 170)
		end;
	switch( isbegin_quest(5932) ) {
	case 0:
		cloakoffnpcself( "#Directing Warp" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Greedy Man#E18_1" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Abused Child#E18_2" );

		mes "- A man quien appears to be greedy";
		mes "- Le esta gritando a un nino.";
		next;
		if (select( "Listen to que es happening.", "Ignore it." ) == 2)
			end;
		emotion ET_FRET, getnpcid(0,"Greedy Man#E18_1");
		npctalk "Man: Es esto unll I obtener in return despues feeding y sheltering a tribal child con nowhere else to ir?", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		emotion ET_SURPRISE, playerattached();
		unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : ??", bc_self;
		next;
		emotion ET_ANGER, getnpcid(0,"Greedy Man#E18_1");
		npctalk "Man: Huh?! Esto esn't incluso funny. Como te atreves lay tu hands on a guest's wallet?", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;
		emotion ET_FRET, getnpcid(0,"Greedy Man#E18_1");
		npctalk "Man: Ugh... Solo estaba heeding el advice alrededor de no to be lenient con el natives.", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Man: Debo tener lost my mente para a moment. Voy a dejar it slide esto tiempo, pero disappear de my sight inmediatamente.", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		specialeffect EF_HIT1, AREA, "Abused Child#E18_2";
		npctalk "Child: Aah!", "Abused Child#E18_2", bc_self;
		emotion ET_PROFUSELY_SWAT, getnpcid(0,"Abused Child#E18_2");
		cloakonnpcself( "Abused Child#E18_2" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Abused Child#E18_3" );
		next;
		emotion ET_STARE, getnpcid(0,"Greedy Man#E18_1");
		npctalk "Man: No puedo tu mantener quiet? Que estas haciendo haciendo such a fuss y yelling all terminado el neighborhood?!", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;
		emotion ET_CRY, getnpcid(0,"Abused Child#E18_3");
		npctalk "Child: I no hice algo. Nunca he visto el guest's wallet.", "Abused Child#E18_3", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Man: Esto... Esto...", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Man: Como dare esto kid talk de vuelta en lugar de begging para forgiveness?!", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Man: Entonces ahora eres lying incluso despues Intente dejar it slide y dar tu forgiveness?", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;
		emotion ET_BLABLA, getnpcid(0,"Greedy Man#E18_1");
		npctalk "Man: No necesito insolent brats como tu alrededor de! Eres fired inmediatamente!", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;
		cloakonnpcself( "Abused Child#E18_3" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Abused Child#E18_2" );
		emotion ET_CRY, getnpcid(0,"Abused Child#E18_2");
		npctalk "Child: Eres wrong, sir.", "Abused Child#E18_2", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Child: Please, do no fire me... Necesitos to earn money para my padre medication.", "Abused Child#E18_2", bc_self;
		next;
		emotion ET_FRET, getnpcid(0,"Greedy Man#E18_1");
		npctalk "Man:?! Medication expenses?", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Man: Eso es tu business. Por que deberia I incluso care?", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Child: Sir, entonces como sobre el money He earned para trabajando hasta ahora?", "Abused Child#E18_2", bc_self;
		next;
		emotion ET_QUESTION, getnpcid(0,"Greedy Man#E18_1");
		npctalk "Man: Que? Money tiene earned?", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;

		switch( isbegin_quest(.@quest_id) ) {
		case 0:
			npctalk "Mejai: Estas planning to collect half flowers?", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;

			case -1:
				break;
			case 0:
			case 1:
				npctalk "Mejai: Oh! Estas seguro?", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
				setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
				sleep2 1000;
				setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
				npctalk "Mejai: Desde hoy no ha ended aun, si pasar to finish tu tasks early hoy, por favor consider helping out at el Flower Room tambien.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
				end;
			case 2:
				break;
			}
			setquest .@quest_id;
			npctalk "Mejai: Por favor se careful of el half flowers blooming near el trees hoy.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
			sleep2 1000;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
			npctalk "Mejai: Alright! Fighting! Bring de vuelta algunos fresh half flowers con vigor hoy.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			end;
		case 1:
			if (rentalcountitem(1000407) < 1) {
				npctalk "Mejai: Por favor continua tu hard work para el fresh Half Flowers hoy.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
				end;
			}
			delitem 1000407,1;	// Eps18_Half_Flower
			erasequest .@quest_id;
			if (checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) == 2)
				erasequest 5937;
			setquest 5937;
			getitem 1000405,3;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
			getexp 18252408,2000000;
			npctalk "Mejai: Tiene worked hard hoy tambien. Please irse el rest to me.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			end;
		case 2:
			end;	// impossible
		}
		end;
	case 3:
		npctalk "Mejai: Es un service eso Todavia estoy contemplating. Quizas nosotros podria initiate it once el business stabilizes un poco?", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
		sleep2 1000;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
		npctalk "Mejai: Cuando eso tiempo comes, Voy a rely on tu assistance.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5936) == 2 && isbegin_quest(5938) == 0 && isbegin_quest(5939) == 0 && isbegin_quest(5940) == 0 && isbegin_quest(5941) == 0 && checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5936) == 2 && rentalcountitem(1000407) > 0" );
	end;
}

gw_fild01,325,376,1	script	Half Flower#EP18_R01	4_EP18_HALFFLOWER,7,7,{
	if (checkweight(1000407,1) == 0) {
		mes "- Espera un momento !! -";
		mes "- Actualmente estas cargando -";
		mes "- demasiados objetos contigo. -";
		mes "- Por favor intenta de nuevo -";
		mes "- despues de soltar algo de peso. -";
		close;
	}
	for ( .@id = 5938; .@id < 5942; .@id++ ) {
		if (isbegin_quest(.@id) == 1) {
			.@quest_id = .@id;
			break;
		}
	}
	if (.@quest_id == 0) {
		unittalk getcharid(3), "Half flower is blooming", bc_self;
		end;
	}
	// player interacts with the npc according to the quest ID
	// 5938 requires EP18_R (flower near a Rock)
	// 5939 requires EP18_L (?)
	// 5940 requires EP18_T (Tree)
	// 5941 requires EP18_W (Water)
	switch( .@quest_id ) {
	case 5938:
		.@string$ = "EP18_R";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],4,1,4,1;
		break;
	case 5939:
		.@string$ = "EP18_L";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],3,2,3,2;
		break;
	case 5940:
		.@string$ = "EP18_T";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],2,2,2,3;
		break;
	case 5941:
		.@string$ = "EP18_W";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],1,4,1,4;
		break;
	}
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2), .@string$) == false) {
		cloakonnpc strnpcinfo(0), getcharid(0);
		end;
	}
	if (rentalcountitem(1000407) > 0) {
		mes "- el Half Flower is mas";
		mes "- no necesario, antes wither";
		mes "- Bring it to Mejai.";
		close;
	}
	.@time = 86400;
	mes "- El Half Flower is in bloom. Vamos a";
	mes "- gather el required materials de el";
	mes "- harvesting tool provided by Mejai.";
	next;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : Que angle deberia I cut el stem?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select("Cut at 10Â¡Ã angle", "Cut at 20Â¡Ã angle", "Cut at 30Â¡Ã angle", "Cut at 40Â¡Ã angle" ) != .@good_answer[0])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : Como muchos leaves deberia I irse?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select("Leave 2 leaves", "Leave 4 leaves", "Leave 6 leaves", "Leave 8 leaves" ) != .@good_answer[1])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : Como muchos milliliters of agua deberia I poner in el small bottle?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select("Poner in 20 ml", "Poner in 30 ml", "Poner in 40 ml", "Poner in 50 ml" ) != .@good_answer[2])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : Como muchos grams of secret powder deberia I poner in?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select( "Poner in 2 grams.", "Poner in 4 grams.", "Poner in 6 grams.", "Poner in 8 grams." ) != .@good_answer[3])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	cloakonnpcself();
	// note: the timer decreases according to the answer but no idea of the ratio
	rentitem 1000407, .@time;	// Ep18_Half_Flower
	mes "- Tienes harvested el Half Flower.";
	mes "- Vamos a entregar it to Mejai antes it withers.";
	close;

OnTouch:
	for ( .@id = 5938; .@id < 5942; .@id++ ) {
		if (isbegin_quest(.@id) == 1) {
			.@quest_id = .@id;
			break;
		}
	}
	if (.@quest_id == 0)
		end;
	setarray .@string$[5938], "EP18_R", "EP18_L", "EP18_T", "EP18_W";
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2), .@string$[.@quest_id]) == false) {
		cloakonnpc strnpcinfo(0), getcharid(0);
		end;
	}
	end;
}

	end;
OnMove:
	while(1) {
		switch( .move ) {
		case 0:
			.val = !.val;
			setarray .@a[0],147,137;
			setarray .@b[0],147,138;
			break;
		case 1:
			setarray .@a[0],148,127;
			setarray .@b[0],147,127;
			break;
		case 2:
			setarray .@a[0],159,115;
			setarray .@b[0],160,115;
			break;
		case 3:
			setarray .@a[0],159,104;
			setarray .@b[0],160,104;
			break;
		case 4:
			setarray .@a[0],159,93;
			setarray .@b[0],160,93;
			break;
		case 5:
			setarray .@a[0],160,82;
			setarray .@b[0],161,82;
			break;
		case 6:
			.val = !.val;
			setarray .@a[0],161,71;
			setarray .@b[0],162,71;
			break;
		}
		.move = .move + .sens[.val];
		unitstopwalk getnpcid(0),USW_FORCE_STOP;
		unitstopwalk getnpcid(0,"Goat#wms03"),USW_FORCE_STOP;
		unitwalk getnpcid(0),.@a[0],.@a[1];
		unitwalk getnpcid(0,"Goat#wms03"),.@b[0],.@b[1];
		sleep 6000;
	}
	end;
OnInit:
	npcspeed 300;
	setarray .sens[0],1,-1;
	.move = 1;
	donpcevent strnpcinfo(0) + "::OnMove";
	end;
}

// Storage and unlock reputation feature
wolfvill,167,135,3	script	Asad#asad	4_EP18_GW_MAN01,{
	if (checkweight(1201,1) == 0) {
		mes "- Espera un momento !! -";
		mes "- Actualmente estas cargando -";
		mes "- demasiados objetos contigo. -";
		mes "- Por favor intenta de nuevo -";
		mes "- despues de soltar algo de peso. -";
		close;
	}
	.@reputation = get_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18);
	if (.@reputation < 1000) {
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Sir, es sido a mientras desde He visto tu.";
		mes "Once tu obtener somewhat familiar con el aldea, venir encontrar me.";
		mes "Voy a offer tu algunos conveniences.";
		next;
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Pero eso es solo if eres^4d4dffva a participate in el tournament^000000.";
		mes "No solo do favors para alguien.";
		next;
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Si mirar closely, ahi va a be places donde a helping mano is necesitaba.";
		mes "By lending a mano, vas a earn algunos goodwill among el gente, I reckon.";
		mes "Dar it tu best shot.";
		close;
	}

	if (.@reputation >= 3000) {
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Welcome, my wolf-like " + strcharinfo(0) + ".";
		mes "Que do necesitas?";
		mes "Oh, He estado mirando para a better smelting box.";
		mes "Te gustaria exchange it para amethyst?";
	}
	else {
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Welcome, Sir.";
		mes "Te gustaria tidy up algunos things?";
		mes "O haria tu como to browse a traves de algunos items Tengo para sale?";
	}
	next;
	.@amethyst_name$ = getitemname(1000405);
	if (.@reputation >= 4000)
		.@menu$[2] = "Exchange Smelting Box for " + .@amethyst_name$;
	else if (.@reputation >= 3000)
		.@menu$[1] = "Exchange Smelting Box for " + .@amethyst_name$;
	if (.@reputation >= 2000)
		.@menu$[0] = "Request space movement.";
	switch( select( "Use el Storage.", "Use el store.", .@menu$[0], .@menu$[1], .@menu$[2] ) ) {
	case 1:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "El Storage fee is 200z.";
		mes "Cual Storage haria tu como to open?";
		next;
		select( "Storage 1" );
		if (Zeny < 200)
			end;
		Zeny -= 200;
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Alright, Voy a open el Storage.";
		mes "Use it bueno.";
		close2;
		openstorage();
		end;
	case 2:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Estas va a sell items?";
		mes "Estas va a buy que necesitas?";
		next;
		switch( select( "Buy items.", "Sell items." ) ) {
		case 1:
			mes "[Asad]";
			mes "No estoy seguro de si Tengo todo necesitas.";
			mes "Pero whatever es, puedes count on me.";
			close2;
			callshop "Tool Dealer#wolf",1;
			end;
		case 2:
			mes "[Asad]";
			mes "Alright. Voy a sell tu algo at a reasonable price.";
			close2;
			callshop "Tool Dealer#wolf",2;
			end;
		}
		end;
	case 3:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Do quieres to teleport to Rachel for 1 " + .@amethyst_name$ + " ?";
		next;
		if (select( "No", "Si" ) == 1)
			end;
		if (countitem(1000405) < 1) {
			mes "[Asad]";
			mes "No tienes suficiente material.";
			close;
		}
		delitem 1000405,1;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
		warp "rachel",115,125;
		end;
	case 4:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Amethyst shop?";
		mes "Puedes exchange ellos para a smelting box..";
		close2;
		callshop "barter_ep18_2",1;
		end;
	case 5:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Amethyst shop?";
		mes "Puedes exchange ellos para a smelting box..";
		close2;
		callshop "barter_ep18_3",1;
		end;
	}
	end;
}

			item_enchant(3);
			end;
